# OSM Table Tennis Location Counts

A clean, country-by-country view of the staged OpenStreetMap table-tennis venue import. Countries are sorted by total venue locations, and cities inside each country are sorted from largest to smallest.

> Source: `supabase/seeds/osm/*.sql`, generated from OpenStreetMap via Overture Maps and GeoNames. Co-located same-name tables are merged into one venue row and preserved in `tables_count`.

## At a Glance

| Metric | Count |
|---|---:|
| Venue locations | 33,330 |
| Tables represented | 44,917 |
| Countries | 44 |
| Cities | 10,291 |
